L /// The behavior of binary detection while searching.
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 53 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
L /// Binary detection is the process of _heuristically_ identifying whether a
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 76 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// given chunk of data is binary or not, and then taking an action based on
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 76 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// the result of that heuristic. The motivation behind detecting binary data
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 77 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// is that binary data often indicates data that is undesirable to search
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 74 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// using textual patterns. Of course, there are many cases in which this isn't
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 79 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// true, which is why binary detection is disabled by default.
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 63 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
L /// Unfortunately, binary detection works differently depending on the type of
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 78 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// search being executed:
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 26 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
L /// 1. When performing a search using a fixed size buffer, binary detection is
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 78 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///    applied to the buffer's contents as it is filled. Binary detection must
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 78 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///    be applied to the buffer directly because binary files may not contain
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 77 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///    line terminators, which could result in exorbitant memory usage.
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 71 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L /// 2. When performing a search using memory maps or by reading data off the
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 76 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///    heap, then binary detection is only guaranteed to be applied to the
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 74 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///    parts corresponding to a match. When `Quit` is enabled, then the first
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 77 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust
L ///    few KB of the data are searched for binary data.
T 3 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust
T 55 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust

T 4 source.rust

T 4 source.rust

T 4 source.rust
T 7 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust

T 52 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust

T 4 source.rust
T 7 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust

T 4 source.rust
T 7 source.rust comment.line.documentation.rust punctuation.definition.comment.rust

T 4 source.rust

T 4 source.rust

T 4 source.rust

T 4 source.rust
